    60→
    61→## 4. 相关现状
    62→
    63→### 4.1 配置页组件（`pages/settings/ApiSetPage.ets`）【代码】
    64→- 状态：`@StorageProp(API_URL) customApiUrl`、`@StorageProp(VALID_API_URL) validApiUrl`（`:14-19`）。
    65→- 状态行：`Text(){ Span('用户云服务状态: ') Span('正常'|'不可用') }`（`:25-35`），UI 树里是一整串 `用户云服务状态: 正常`。【实测】
    66→- 表单：只有一个 `TextInput({ text: this.customApiUrl })`（`:66-69`）+ `Button('保存配置')`（`:70-74`）。
    67→- 保存：`setCustomApiUrl()`（`:133-138`）= `AppStorage.setOrCreate(API_URL)` + `PreferencesUtils.putPreferenceValue(SET_PERF, API_URL)` + `ToastUtil.showToast('配置已保存, 正在检查云服务是否可用...')` + `checkValidUrl()`（不 await、没有在途保护）。
    68→- 可用时额外显示 `(高级) 手动设置Cookie` 区（`:37-39`、`:81-125`）。
    69→
    70→### 4.2 状态模型 / 本地存储【代码】
    71→- 键：`StorageConstants.API_URL = 'apiUrl'`（`common/constants/StorageConstants.ets:271`，AppStorage + Preference）；`VALID_API_URL = 'isValidApiUrl'`（`:44`，只在 AppStorage）；首选项文件名 `SET_PERF = 'Settings'`（`:198`）。
    72→- 启动加载：`initSettings()` → `handleSetting<string>(API_URL, '')`（`common/functions/app/settings.ets:21-23`），没有就写默认值 `''`，再 `AppStorage.setOrCreate`（`:80-93`）。通用写法 `changeSettings(name, value)`（`:70-78`）。
    73→- 本机模拟器上首选项文件 `/data/app/el2/100/base/cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic/haps/entry/preferences/Settings` 是 XML，可在 shell 下读到，便于自测核对持久化。【实测】
    74→- 使用 `isValidApiUrl` 的 UI：`Explore.ets:20`（默认 true）、`IndexRecommend.ets:24`（默认 true）、`Me.ets:14`（默认 true）、`ApiSetPage.ets:17`（默认 false）、`ApiDialog.ets:11`。【代码】
    75→
    76→### 4.3 网络服务【代码】
    77→- `common/utils/request/RequestUtil.ets:11-13` axios 实例，超时 15000ms；请求拦截器 `:15-29`：`baseURL = params.baseUrl || getApiUrl()`（`getApiUrl` 读 AppStorage `apiUrl`，`common/functions/common/get.ets:14-16`），追加 `cookie`、`realIP` 参数；不支持认证头。`commonRequest` 返回 `response.data`（`:46-49`）。
    78→- 连通性检查：`checkValidUrl()`（`common/functions/common/get.ets:40-57`）`GET /inner/version`，`code === 200` → `isValidApiUrl=true`，否则或异常 → false；没有并发控制，旧请求晚到会覆盖新结果。
    79→- 启动顺序：`EntryAbility.ets:24-30` 先加载 3 个首选项再 `await appInit()`；`appInit()`（`common/functions/app/appInit.ets:26-40`）：`initSettings` → … → `getAppInfo()`（请求外网 `https://api.chenlvin.cc/api/cloudmusic/app`，`Constants.ets:8`、`api/chenlvinApi.ets:10-21`，失败 Toast `获取应用信息失败`）→ `checkValidUrl()`（`:35`）→ 可用才 `initUserInfo()`（登录态）。
    80→- 在线内容接口：`getTopList()` `GET /toplist`（`api/topListApi.ets:9-16`，成功写首选项缓存）；`getPersonalizedList()` `GET /personalized`（`api/playListApi.ets:137-145`，写 AppStorage + 首选项）。
    81→
    82→### 4.4 发现页状态（`common/view/Explore.ets`）【代码】
    83→- `isLogged` 带 `@Watch('getContent')`（`:19`）——**只有登录态变化才拉内容**；`validApiUrl` 没有 Watch（`:20`）。
    84→- `getTopLists()`（`:47-54`）只在缓存为空且可用时请求；`getPersonalizedList()`（`:56-63`）还要求已登录。两者都没有 try/finally，请求抛错时 `isLoadingTopList` / `isLoadingPersonalized` 会停在 true（骨架屏不消失）。
    85→- 下拉刷新 `refreshContent`（`:65-83`）：5s 冷却，不检查 `validApiUrl`，未登录也会请求 `/toplist` 和 `/personalized`，成功 Toast `刷新成功`。
    86→- 缓存：启动时从 `UserPreference` 读 `topLists`、`personalizedList` 进 AppStorage（`appInit.ets:76-77,85-86`）。
    87→
    88→### 4.5 本地曲库（`pages/local/LocalSongPage.ets`）【代码】
    89→- 状态 `:25-36`；`aboutToAppear` `:70-78`（loading → `refreshList()` → `FileUtils.getFileName()`）；`refreshList` `:260-267`（RDB `getAllSongs()` 倒序）。
    90→- 上传：菜单 → `DialogUtil.showPrimaryDialog`（标题 `上传文件`、按钮 `取消`/`上传`，`:50-65`）→ `handleUpload` `:269-281` → `PickerUtil.selectDocument`（`.mp3/.flac`，最多 99）→ `FileUtils.saveFilesToDirectory`（`common/utils/file/FileUtils.ets:155-186`：同名已存在跳过；复制到 `filesDir`；`fetchSongInfo` 按 `歌手 - 歌名.ext` 解析；写 RDB）→ Toast `上传 N 个文件` → `refreshList()`。
    91→- 数据：RDB `LocalSongs.db` 表 `songs`（`common/utils/data/RelationalStoreUtils.ets:11,25-35`）。
    92→
    93→### 4.6 实机操作记录（2026-10-18，本次安装的基线 HAP）【实测】
    94→1. 冷启动（模拟器上原有配置 `apiUrl=http://172.24.16.249:3000`，地址已失效）：约 6s 时发现页仍显示 `每日推荐`/`音乐云盘`/`推荐歌单`/`排行榜`；约 20s 后（15s 超时后）只剩问候语 + `本地曲库` 卡片。
    95→2. 配置页：`用户云服务状态: 不可用`，输入框回显旧地址。把输入改成 `http://10.0.2.2:3000` → `保存配置` → Toast `配置已保存, 正在检查云服务是否可用...` → 约 1s 内状态变为 `用户云服务状态: 正常`，出现 `(高级) 手动设置Cookie` + Toggle；服务端日志 `[OK] /inner/version?cookie=&realIP=116.25.146.177`。
    96→3. 关闭面板切到发现页：出现 `推荐歌单`、`排行榜` 标题，但**没有任何内容请求**，两块都是空的（只有 `更多排行`）。
    97→4. 已配置、未登录，冷启动：服务端只收到 `/inner/version`、`/login/status`，**没有 `/toplist`**；发现页同第 3 步。
    98→5. 下拉刷新：服务端收到 `/toplist` 和 `/personalized`，推荐歌单卡片出现（如 `自然舒心 | 宁静 柔和 清新 明亮 自然`）——未登录也能拿到推荐歌单。
    99→6. 清空输入框后保存：Toast 照常，状态 `不可用`，服务端**无请求**。随后冷启动：1.5s 时发现页就只有问候语 + `本地曲库` 卡片，没有引导；`我的` 页只有 `应用设置`、`本地曲库`，没有 `登录`。
   100→7. 本地曲库（空）：显示 `本地文件列表为空` + `迁移旧版本数据`。点上传 → 弹窗 → `取消` 无变化；`上传` → 系统选择器（`最近` 页签显示 `没有文件`）→ 返回取消，列表不变、无 Toast。
   101→8. 把测试文件 `测试歌手 - 测试歌曲.mp3` 用 `hdc file send` 放到 `/storage/media/100/local/files/Docs/Download/` → 上传 → 选择器 `浏览` → `我的手机` → `Download` → 单击文件即返回 → Toast `上传 1 个文件`，列表 `共 1 首歌曲`，卡片 `测试歌曲` / `测试歌手`。场景三第 3 步在基线已可用。
   102→9. 还原：多选 → 删除 → `删除` 确认，列表回到 `本地文件列表为空`；设备上的测试 mp3 已删除；`apiUrl` 已通过配置页改回原值 `http://172.24.16.249:3000`。

   108→- 全局状态联动：所有页面都用 `@StorageProp(VALID_API_URL)` 订阅，`checkValidUrl()` 写一次全局生效。发现页"配置成功后加载内容"可以在 `Explore` 上给 `validApiUrl` 加 `@Watch`，复用 `getTopList()` / `getPersonalizedList()` 与 `refreshContent` 的拉取逻辑。
   109→- 底部面板：`bindSheet(@State 布尔, @Builder, SheetOptions{ preferType: SheetType.BOTTOM, onDisappear: 复位 })`，同类写法见 `pages/art/AlbumPage.ets:34-41`、`components/buttons/play/PlayQueueButton.ets:16`；设置面板本身 `Me.ets:24-32,126`。

   111→- 样式：`ApiSetPage.ets:150-171` 的 `itemStyle` / `LeftText` / `setButton`。
   112→- Toast：`ToastUtil.s

+  if (!isApiHostExist) {
+    const legacyConfig = parseApiUrl(AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_URL) ?? '')
+    if (legacyConfig) {
+      await putApiFields(legacyConfig)
+    }
+  }
+
   // 处理关联配置项（API URL）
   const results = await Promise.all([
     PreferencesUtils.hasPreferenceValue(SetPerf, StorageConstants.IS_CUSTOM_COOKIE),
@@ -90,4 +107,100 @@ async function handleSetting<T extends preferences.ValueType>(
     await PreferencesUtils.putPreferenceValue(storageArea, key, defaultValue)
   }
   AppStorage.setOrCreate(key, value)
+}
+
+/**
+ * 当前在线服务配置
+ */
+export function getApiConfig(): ApiConfig {
+  const config: ApiConfig = {
+    protocol: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_PROTOCOL) ?? 'http',
+    host: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_HOST) ?? '',
+    port: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_PORT) ?? '',
+    path: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_PATH) ?? '',
+    user: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_USER) ?? '',
+    password: AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_PASSWORD) ?? ''
+  }
+  return config
+}
+
+/**
+ * 规范化在线服务配置
+ * 去除首尾空格; 主机去掉协议前缀与末尾斜杠; 非法端口(非数字/超出1-65535)视为空; 路径统一为 '/xxx' 或 ''
+ */
+export function normalizeApiConfig(config: ApiConfig): ApiConfig {
+  const host = config.host.trim().replace(/^https?:\/\//i, '').replace(/\/+$/, '')
+  let port = config.port.trim()
+  const portNum = Number(port)
+  port = /^\d+$/.test(port) && portNum >= 1 && portNum <= 65535 ? portNum.toString() : ''
+  const path = config.path.trim().replace(/^\/+/, '').replace(/\/+$/, '')
+  const normalized: ApiConfig = {
+    protocol: config.protocol.trim().toLowerCase() === 'https' ? 'https' : 'http',
+    host: host,
+    port: port,
+    path: path ? '/' + path : '',
+    user: config.user.trim(),
+    password: config.password
+  }
+  return normalized
+}
+
+/**
+ * 组合 API 基础地址: {协议}://{主机}[:{端口}]{路径}，主机为空时返回 ''
+ */
+export function buildApiUrl(config: ApiConfig): string {
+  const c = normalizeApiConfig(config)
+  if (!c.host) {
+    return ''
+  }
+  return `${c.protocol}://${c.host}${c.port ? ':' + c.port : ''}${c.path}`
+}
+
+/**
+ * 解析单字符串 API 地址，无法解析时返回 undefined
+ */
+export function parseApiUrl(url: string): ApiConfig | undefined {
+  const match = /^(https?):\/\/([^\/:?#\s]+)(?::(\d+))?(\/[^?#\s]*)?$/i.exec(url.trim())
+  if (!match) {
+    return undefined
+  }
+  const config: ApiConfig = {
+    protocol: match[1],
+    host: match[2],
+    port: match[3] ?? '',
+    path: match[4] ?? '',
+    user: '',
+    password: ''
+  }
+  return normalizeApiConfig(config)
+}
+
+/**
+ * 保存在线服务配置并检查连通性
+ * 设置页与发现页配置面板共用
+ */
+export function saveApiConfig(config: ApiConfig): Promise<boolean> {
+  const normalized = normalizeApiConfig(config)
+  const apiUrl = buildApiUrl(normalized)
+  putApiFields(normalized)
+  putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_URL, apiUrl)
+  ToastUtil.showToast('配置已保存，正在检查服务是否可用…')
+  return checkValidUrl()
+}
+
+function putApiFields(config: ApiConfig): Promise<void[]> {
+  return Promise.all([
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_PROTOCOL, config.protocol),
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_HOST, config.host),
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_PORT, config.port),
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_PATH, config.path),
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_USER, config.user),
+    putApiSetting(StorageConstants.API_PASSWORD, config.password)
+  ])
+}
+
+// 先同步写 AppStorage（后续请求立即使用新值），再持久化
+function putApiSetting(key: string, value: string): Promise<void> {
+  AppStorage.setOrCreate(key, value)
+  return PreferencesUtils.putPreferenceValue(StorageConstants.SET_PERF, key, value)
 }

+      checkingKey = undefined

+export function getApiAuthorization(): string {
+  const user = AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_USER) ?? ''
+  const password = AppStorage.get<string>(StorageConstants.API_PASSWORD) ?? ''
+  if (!user && !password) {
+    return ''
+  }
+  const bytes = new util.TextEncoder().encodeInto(`${user}:${password}`)
+  return 'Basic ' + new util.Base64Helper().encodeToStringSync(bytes)
+}

    35→
    36→class RecordingProxy:
    37→    """记录请求的转发代理；/inner/version 可人为延迟，用于构造“检查在途”。"""
    38→
    39→    def __init__(self, port: int = PROXY_PORT, upstream: str = UPSTREAM):
    40→        self.requests: list[tuple[str, str | None]] = []
    41→        self.delay = 0.0
    42→        proxy = self
    43→
    44→        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    45→            def do_GET(self):  # noqa: N802
    46→                proxy.requests.append((self.path, self.headers.get("Authorization")))
    47→                if "/inner/version" in self.path and proxy.delay:
    48→                    time.sleep(proxy.delay)
    49→                try:
    50→                    with urllib.request.urlopen(upstream + self.path, timeout=10) as resp:
    51→                        body = resp.read()
    52→                except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
    53→                    body = b'{"code":500}'
    54→                self.send_response(200)
    55→                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
    56→                self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
    57→                self.end_headers()
    58→                self.wfile.write(body)
    59→
    60→            def log_message(self, *args):
    61→                pass
    62→
    63→        self.httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", port), Handler)
    64→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self.httpd.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    65→
    66→    def __enter__(self):
    67→        self.thread.start()
    68→        return self
    69→
    70→    def __exit__(self, *exc):
    71→        self.httpd.shutdown()
    72→        self.httpd.server_close()
    73→
    74→    def clear(self) -> None:
    75→        self.requests.clear()
    76→
    77→    def paths(self, prefix: str) -> list[str]:
    78→        """以 prefix 开头（忽略查询串）的请求路径。"""
    79→        return [p for p, _ in self.requests if p.split("?", 1)[0].endswith(prefix)]
    80→
    81→    def auth_of(self, prefix: str) -> list[str | None]:
    82→        return [a for p, a in self.requests if p.split("?", 1)[0].endswith(prefix)]
    83→
    84→
    85→def basic_auth(user: str, password: str) -> str:
    86→    return "Basic " + base64.b64encode(f"{user}:{password}".encode()).decode()

   118→
   119→    def close_settings(self) -> None:
   120→        """从配置页返回到设置列表，再关闭设置面板。"""
   121→        self.driver.press_back()
   122→        self.driver.wait(1)
   123→        if self.find_or_none(BY.text("应用设置"), timeout=2):
   124→            self.driver.press_back()
   125→            self.driver.wait(1)
   126→
   127→    def input_value(self, key: str) -> str:
   128→        comp = self.require_component(BY.key(key), f"未找到输入框 {key}")
   129→        return comp.getText() or ""
   130→
   131→    def fill(self, key: str, value: str) -> None:
   132→        comp = self.require_component(BY.key(key), f"未找到输入框 {key}")
   133→        comp.clearText()
   134→        if value:
   135→            comp.inputText(value)
   136→        self.driver.wait(0.3)
   137→
   138→    def fill_form(self, host: str = "", port: str = "", protocol: str = "HTTP", path: str = "",
   139→                  user: str = "", password: str = "") -> None:
   140→        for key, value in zip(FIELDS, (host, port, path, user, password)):
   141→            self.fill(key, value)
   142→        self.click_key("api_protocol_https" if protocol == "HTTPS" else "api_protocol_http")
   143→
   144→    def save(self) -> str | None:
   145→        """点击“保存配置”，返回捕获到的 Toast 文本。"""
   146→        self.driver.start_listen_toast()
   147→        self.click_key("api_save_button")
   148→        return self.driver.get_latest_toast(timeout=5)
   149→
   150→    def status(self) -> str:
   151→        return self.require_component(BY.key("api_status"), "未找到连通性状态").getText()
   152→
   153→    def wait_status(self, expected: str, timeout: float = 20) -> str:
   154→        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   155→        value = self.status()
   156→        while value != expected and time.monotonic() < deadline:
   157→            self.driver.wait(0.5)
   158→            value = self.status()
   159→        return value
   160→
   161→    def configure(self, host: str, port: str = "", **kwargs) -> None:
   162→        """冷启动并经设置页保存一份配置，结束后回到发现页。"""
   163→        self.restart()
   164→        self.open_api_page()
   165→        self.fill_form(host=host, port=port, **kwargs)
   166→        self.save()
   167→        self.driver.wait(2)
   168→        self.close_settings()
   169→        self.click_text("发现")
   170→
</file>

结果：

````text
<file>
     1→# test-harness：通用自测框架
     2→
     3→目标：为每道题提供**统一**的设备连接、环境校验、Hypium 封装和测试运行/报告方式；
     4→各题自己的测试点（断言逻辑）单独编写，放在各题工程的 `hypium_tests/` 下，不属于本框架。
     5→
     6→本框架不是官方评测器，只用于本地自测，帮助在提交前用同款工具链（Hypium + hdc）验证功能，
     7→降低"本地通过、正式评测翻车"的概率。官方评测环境规格（Pura 90 Pro Max 模拟器、HarmonyOS 6.1.0 / API 23、
     8→1308 × 2880 / 560 dpi）已内置在 `harness/env_check.py` 中。
     9→
    10→## 目录结构
    11→
    12→```
    13→test-harness/
    14→├── README.md                本文件
    15→├── requirements.txt         框架自身的 Python 依赖（hypium==6.1.0.210）
    16→└── harness/                 通用库，与赛题无关
    17→    ├── __init__.py
    18→    ├── device.py             设备发现、选择、连接（封装 hdc / UiDriver.connect）
    19→    ├── env_check.py          环境校验：对照官方模拟器规格，检查屏幕分辨率/dpi/系统版本
    20→    ├── driver.py             HarnessTestCase：带超时重试的查找/等待/断言辅助方法
    21→    ├── window.py             按窗口名从 hidumper 读取 [x y w h]（悬浮窗等不在 UI 树里的窗口）
    22→    ├── app.py                应用生命周期与导航：restart_app、click_text_path
    23→    ├── runner.py             测试运行器：发现并执行某道题工程下的测试文件，汇总报告
    24→    └── report.py             统一的结果报告：pass / fail / error / skip 分开统计，skip 不算通过
    25→```
    26→
    27→## 使用方式
    28→
    29→以下命令中 `COMMON` 指工作区的 `common/` 绝对路径，在题目工程目录里可用 `COMMON="$(cd ../common && pwd)"` 得到。
    30→需要 `hdc` 在 PATH 中（DevEco 的 `sdk/default/openharmony/toolchains`）。
    31→
    32→### 1. 环境（每台机器建一次）
    33→
    34→`$COMMON/.venv` 不进仓库，按 `requirements.txt` 在本机创建：
    35→
    36→```bash
    37→python3 -m venv "$COMMON/.venv"
    38→"$COMMON/.venv/bin/python" -m pip install -r "$COMMON/test-harness/requirements.txt" \
    39→    --index-url https://repo.huaweicloud.com/repository/pypi/simple
    40→```
    41→
    42→### 2. 校验模拟器是否匹配官方规格
    43→
    44→```bash
    45→PYTHONPATH="$COMMON/test-harness" "$COMMON/.venv/bin/python" -m harness.env_check
    46→```
    47→
    48→不一致会给出告警，但不阻塞自测。
    49→
    50→### 3. 跑某道题的测试
    51→
    52→**先安装本题刚构建的 HAP。** 同一上游项目的多道题共用同一个 bundleName，
    53→模拟器上装的可能是别的题的产物，直接跑会测错对象。
    54→
    55→```bash
    56→cd <本题工程目录>/logs        # Hypium 会在当前目录生成 reports/，在 logs/ 下运行避免污染工程根目录
    57→PYTHONPATH="$COMMON/test-harness" "$COMMON/.venv/bin/python" -m harness.runner \
    58→    --project "$(cd .. && pwd)" --device <设备序列号> --json "$PWD/selftest.json"
    59→```
    60→
    61→`runner` 会：
    62→1. 调用 `env_check` 做一次环境校验并记录在报告头部（不阻塞；`--skip-env-check` 可跳过）
    63→2. 自动发现 `<project>/hypium_tests/test_*.py` 下的所有测试
    64→3. 执行并输出统一格式的通过/失败报告（终端 + 可选 JSON 文件）
    65→
    66→### 4. 给本题写测试点
    67→
    68→1. 在本题工程下建 `hypium_tests/__init__.py` 与 `hypium_tests/test_<场景>.py`
    69→2. 测试类继承 `harness.driver.HarnessTestCase`，可直接使用：
    70→   `self.driver`、`self.find_or_none()`、`self.require_component()`、`self.require_text()`、
    71→   `self.require_type()`、`self.require_precondition()`（前置不满足记为 skip，不算通过）
    72→3. 只写本题 Spec 描述的断言逻辑，不用关心设备连接、重试、报告格式
    73→
    74→建议在被测组件上设置稳定的 `.id()`，测试里用 `BY.key("<id>")` 定位，比按文本或类型+顺序定位更稳。
    75→
</file>

**[1.4 工具] `read_file`** · toolState=executed · id=toolu_bdrk_017TmaPmTf43ST9eLRzoXCxw

结果：

```text
<file>
     1→"""UiDriver 的薄封装基类：带超时重试的查找/等待/断言辅助方法。
     2→
     3→各题的测试用例应继承 `HarnessTestCase`，只编写该题 spec 描述的断言逻辑；
     4→设备连接、重试查找、报告格式等通用部分由本框架负责。
     5→"""
     6→from __future__ import annotations
     7→
     8→import time
     9→import unittest
    10→
    11→from hypium import BY, UiDriver  # type: ignore[import-not-found]
    12→
    13→from . import device as device_mod
    14→
    15→DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 15
    16→DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL = 0.5
    17→
    18→
    19→class HarnessTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    20→    """所有题目的 Hypium 测试类的通用基类。
    21→
    22→    子类可直接使用 `self.driver`（已连接的 UiDriver）、
    23→    `self.require_text()`、`self.require_component()`、`self.find_or_none()`。
    24→    设备序列号通过类属性 `device_sn` 传入（由 runner 负责设置），
    25→    若单独运行某个测试文件，也可以设置环境变量 HDC_DEVICE 或在命令行传 --device。
    26→    """
    27→
    28→    device_sn: str | None = None
    29→
    30→    @classmethod
    31→    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
    32→        sn = cls.device_sn or device_mod.select_device(None)
    33→        cls.driver = UiDriver.connect(device_sn=sn)
    34→        cls.driver.set_implicit_wait_time(5)
    35→
    36→    def find_or_none(self, by, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
    37→        """按 `by` 条件轮询查找组件，超时返回 None（不触发失败）。"""
    38→        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    39→        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    40→            component = self.driver.find_component(by)
    41→            if component is not None:
    42→                return component
    43→            self.driver.wait(DEFAULT_POLL_INTERVAL)
    44→        return None
    45→
    46→    def require_component(self, by, message: str, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
    47→        """按 `by` 条件轮询查找组件，超时则用 `message` 调用 self.fail()。"""
    48→        component = self.find_or_none(by, timeout=timeout)
    49→        if component is None:
    50→            self.fail(message)
    51→        return component
    52→
    53→    def require_text(self, text: str, message: str, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
    54→        """查找包含指定文本的组件，等价于题包 Hypium 示例中的用法。"""
    55→        return self.require_component(BY.text(text), message, timeout=timeout)
    56→
    57→    def require_type(self, component_type: str, message: str, timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT):
    58→        """查找指定类型（如 'List'、'Slider'、'Toggle'）的组件。"""
    59→        return self.require_component(BY.type(component_type), message, timeout=timeout)
    60→
    61→    def require_precondition(self, condition: bool, message: str) -> None:
    62→        """前置条件不满足时记为 skip（未验证），而不是通过或失败。"""
    63→        if not condition:
    64→            self.skipTest(f"前置条件未满足: {message}")
    65→
    66→    @staticmethod
    67→    def slider_value(slider) -> float:
    68→        """读取 Slider 当前值（UI dump 里 Slider 的 text 即其数值，如 '18.000000'）。"""
    69→        return float(slider.getText())
    70→
    71→    def sliders(self) -> list:
    72→        """当前页面所有 Slider，按页面从上到下的顺序。"""
    73→        return self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type("Slider")) or []
    74→
    75→    def set_slider(self, index: int, value: float, vmin: float, vmax: float,
    76→                   tolerance: float | None = None, attempts: int = 3) -> float:
    77→        """点击第 index 个 Slider 轨道上对应 value 的位置，返回最终读到的实际值。
    78→
    79→        实测（HarmonyOS 6.1 模拟器）：点击轨道会直接跳值，轨道铺满组件 bounds，位置与数值线性对应；
    80→        刚进入页面后的第一次点击观察到被丢弃过一次，所以读回值与目标不符时会重试。
    81→        实际值取决于被测 Slider 的 step：返回值不等于 value 不代表 harness 出错，
    82→        可能正是被测应用的 Slider 取不到该值，调用方应对返回值做断言。
    83→        """
    84→        tol = tolerance if tolerance is not None else (vmax - vmin) * 0.02
    85→        actual = self.slider_value(self.sliders()[index])
    86→        for _ in range(attempts):
    87→            b = self.sliders()[index].getBounds()
    88→            frac = (value - vmin) / (vmax - vmin)
    89→            x = min(max(int(b.left + frac * (b.right - b.left)), b.left + 2), b.right - 2)
    90→            self.driver.click((x, (b.top + b.bottom) // 2))
    91→            self.driver.wait(0.8)
    92→            actual = self.slider_value(self.sliders()[index])
    93→            if abs(actual - value) <= tol:
    94→                break
    95→        return actual
    96→
    97→    def text_after(self, label: str) -> str | None:
    98→        """页面文本序列里紧跟在 label 之后的那一项（用于读取“标题 + 数值”形式的行）。"""
    99→        texts = [t.getText() for t in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type("Text")) or [])]
   100→        texts = [t for t in texts if t]
   101→        return texts[texts.index(label) + 1] if label in texts else None
   102→
</file>
<metadata>The file has 102 lines in total.</metadata>
```

结果：

```text
<file>
     1→"""测试运行器：发现并执行某道题目录下的测试文件，汇总统一格式的报告。
     2→
     3→用法：
     4→    python -m harness.runner --project <本题工程目录> [--device <序列号>] [--json out.json]
     5→
     6→约定：被测项目目录下的 `hypium_tests/test_*.py` 为该题的测试文件，
     7→其中的测试类应继承 `harness.driver.HarnessTestCase`。
     8→"""
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import argparse
    12→import sys
    13→import unittest
    14→from pathlib import Path
    15→
    16→from . import device as device_mod
    17→from . import env_check
    18→from .report import RecordingResult, RunReport
    19→
    20→
    21→def discover(project_dir: Path) -> unittest.TestSuite:
    22→    tests_dir = project_dir / "hypium_tests"
    23→    if not tests_dir.is_dir():
    24→        raise FileNotFoundError(
    25→            f"未找到测试目录: {tests_dir}；请在该题工作目录下创建 hypium_tests/test_*.py"
    26→        )
    27→    loader = unittest.TestLoader()
    28→    return loader.discover(start_dir=str(tests_dir), pattern="test_*.py", top_level_dir=str(project_dir))
    29→
    30→
    31→def _collect_case_names(suite: unittest.TestSuite) -> list[str]:
    32→    names = []
    33→    for test in suite:
    34→        if isinstance(test, unittest.TestSuite):
    35→            names.extend(_collect_case_names(test))
    36→        else:
    37→            names.append(str(test))
    38→    return names
    39→
    40→
    41→def _inject_device_sn(suite: unittest.TestSuite, device_sn: str) -> None:
    42→    """把设备序列号注入每个 HarnessTestCase 子类，供 setUpClass 使用。"""
    43→    for test in suite:
    44→        if isinstance(test, unittest.TestSuite):
    45→            _inject_device_sn(test, device_sn)
    46→        else:
    47→            test.__class__.device_sn = device_sn
    48→
    49→
    50→def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    51→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    52→    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="本题工程目录（绝对路径）")
    53→    parser.add_argument("--device", default=None, help="设备序列号，省略则要求唯一在线设备")
    54→    parser.add_argument("--json", default=None, help="将报告另存为 JSON 文件的路径")
    55→    parser.add_argument("--skip-env-check", action="store_true", help="跳过环境规格校验")
    56→    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    57→
    58→    project_dir = Path(args.project).resolve()
    59→    device_sn = device_mod.select_device(args.device)
    60→
    61→    env_warnings: list[str] = []
    62→    if not args.skip_env_check:
    63→        env_warnings = env_check.check(device_sn).warnings
    64→
    65→    suite = discover(project_dir)
    66→    case_names = _collect_case_names(suite)
    67→    _inject_device_sn(suite, device_sn)
    68→
    69→    runner = unittest.TextTestRunner(verbosity=2, resultclass=RecordingResult)
    70→    result = runner.run(suite)
    71→
    72→    report = RunReport(
    73→        project=str(project_dir),
    74→        device_sn=device_sn,
    75→        env_warnings=env_warnings,
    76→        cases=result.to_cases(case_names),
    77→    )
    78→    report.print_summary()
    79→
    80→    if args.json:
    81→        Path(args.json).write_text(report.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")
    82→        print(f"\n报告已保存: {args.json}")
    83→
    84→    return 0 if report.failed == 0 else 1
    85→
    86→
    87→if __name__ == "__main__":
    88→    sys.exit(main())
    89→
</file>
<metadata>The file has 89 lines in total.</metadata>
```

**[1.6 推理]**

```text
This looks like a prior debugging session with probe scripts that got stuck repeatedly searching for the api_save_button, possibly because it wasn't clickable, and later ran slow when filling the password field, before being interrupted. I want to check the earlier parts of the 09-08 log to understand what exactly was being probed.


```

Looking past that, the sheet save worked in both the 09:12 and 09:13 runs, but then the password-filling test got interrupted — these were likely probe scripts run inline or via a temp file that no longer exists.

What's left is writing the actual hypium test files, building fresh, installing, running, and fixing issues as they come up. I also want to tidy up the stray reports/ directory left over from earlier probing — rather than delete it outright, I'll move it into logs/ to stay non-destructive. Before writing tests, I should double check the full Explore.ets file along with SettingsPage, ApiSetPage, Me.ets, and IndexRecommend, plus confirm hdc, docker, and the emulator connection are all in order — starting with a full read of Explore.ets.

**[1.8 推理]**

```text
I'm checking the refresh logic handles the valid-to-invalid API URL transition correctly, hiding content and showing the setup guide, and noting that the default validApiUrl state gets overridden during app initialization.

The timing between AppStorage updates and the Explore component's lifecycle matters here — if validApiUrl flips true before the component mounts while appLoading is still true, the aboutToAppear check would skip loading content, but the subsequent onAppLoadingChange handler should catch it once loading finishes, so the sequencing still resolves correctly.

I'm checking the dedup logic for checkValidUrl: using sequence numbers to ignore stale in-flight checks works correctly for interleaved saves, and since putApiSetting writes to AppStorage synchronously, ApiConfigForm's save-then-loadConfig flow reads back the fresh value without a race.

Now I'm looking at the Radio component's checked binding tied to protocol state, and considering how Hypium tests might match on the Radio's text label when distinguishing between the HTTP and HTTPS options.

I'm reviewing remaining UI details — the ApiSetPage title, status label text, and dedupe logic in Settings using getAllPathName — plus the earlier explore sheet issue with the save button not being found, which seems resolved by using SheetSize.LARGE. I'm also confirming that Hypium's BY.key selector matches the component's id property.

There's a potential circular import between settings.ets and get.ets through RequestUtil's commonRequest, but that pattern already existed in baseline so it's not a new issue. I should verify the build reflects the latest source changes after 09:13.

**[1.11 推理]**

```text
The warning count difference is just from incremental build artifacts in oh_modules, not new issues in our code, so I'll do a clean build at the end to confirm. Now I'll plan the test files, making sure to cover first entry, no data, cancel, return and re-enter, and restart scenarios.

For simulating app state, I could clear data with `bm clean -n cc.chenlvin.cloudmusic -d`, though that's destructive to shared emulator state. Setting an empty host through the UI is a safer way to simulate the "unconfigured" case without wiping other test data, so I'll rely on that approach rather than uninstalling and reinstalling fresh.

Fresh install versus the "empty host saved" state likely follow nearly identical code paths, so the risk of skipping it seems low. I consider backing up the app's data directory before uninstalling to allow restoration, but restoring files with proper ownership would be messy, so I'll skip that and instead flag fresh-install as untested in the report, leaving it to the user's judgment rather than risk destructive action.

For the testing approach, I'm using a RecordingProxy on port 3901 forwarding to the docker service on 3000, with the emulator reaching the host via 10.0.2.2:3901 — though binding on the host might run into sandbox network restrictions. I note the proxy always returns HTTP 200 even on upstream failure (with a {"code":500} body), and that /inner/version has a 2-minute cache affecting request counting. I'm now outlining the test file structure, starting with scenario 1's form fields and status test.

Then I'm sketching more scenario 1 tests: validating save behavior with a dead config producing an unavailable status and a toast, then a valid proxy config producing a normal status with exactly one /inner/version hit recorded, followed by a persistence-after-restart test and a rapid-save deduplication test.

For the stale-result test, I need to design two addresses where a slow invalid response (A) is still in flight when a fast valid response (B) completes, so I'm considering using a proxy path like "/slow" that forwards to a 404 upstream with delay, while B hits the normal fast proxy path — ensuring B's valid result isn't overwritten by A's late-arriving invalid one.


```

  "new_string": "class RecordingProxy:\n    \"\"\"记录请求的转发代理；/inner/version 可人为延迟，用于构造“检查在途”。\n\n    delay_prefix 非空时只延迟以该前缀开头的 /inner/version（如配置路径 /slow），其余照常返回。\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, port: int = PROXY_PORT, upstream: str = UPSTREAM):\n        self.requests: list[tuple[str, str | None]] = []\n        self.delay = 0.0\n        self.delay_prefix: str | None = None\n        proxy = self\n\n        class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):\n            def do_GET(self):  # noqa: N802\n                proxy.requests.append((self.path, self.headers.get(\"Authorization\")))\n                if \"/inner/version\" in self.path and proxy.delay and (\n                        not proxy.delay_prefix or self.path.startswith(proxy.delay_prefix)):\n                    time.sleep(proxy.delay)"

  "new_string": "    def configure(self, host: str, port: str = \"\", **kwargs) -> None:\n        \"\"\"冷启动并经设置页保存一份配置，结束后回到发现页。\"\"\"\n        self.restart()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.fill_form(host=host, port=port, **kwargs)\n        self.save()\n        self.driver.wait(2)\n        self.close_settings()\n        self.click_text(\"发现\")\n\n    def wait_until(self, predicate, timeout: float = 20, interval: float = 0.5) -> bool:\n        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout\n        while time.monotonic() < deadline:\n            if predicate():\n                return True\n            self.driver.wait(interval)\n        return predicate()\n\n    def wait_gone(self, by, timeout: float = 10) -> bool:\n        return self.wait_until(lambda: self.driver.find_component(by) is None, timeout=timeout)\n\n    def exists(self, by) -> bool:\n        return self.driver.find_component(by) is not None",

{

  "content": "\"\"\"场景一：设置 → 在线服务 → 在线服务配置（结构化表单、保存提示、连通性检查）。\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom hypium import BY\n\nfrom hypium_tests.cmu_helpers import (DEAD_PORT, HOST, PROXY_PORT, T7, CmuTestCase, RecordingProxy,\n                                      basic_auth)\n\nPORT = str(PROXY_PORT)\n\n\nclass TestS1ApiConfig(CmuTestCase):\n    proxy: RecordingProxy\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.proxy = RecordingProxy().__enter__()\n\n    @classmethod\n    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:\n        cls.proxy.__exit__(None, None, None)\n\n    def setUp(self) -> None:\n        self.proxy.delay = 0.0\n        self.proxy.delay_prefix = None\n\n    def checks(self) -> int:\n        return len(self.proxy.paths(\"/inner/version\"))\n\n    # A1-A9：入口、标题、六项输入、协议选项、状态、保存按钮\n    def test_01_form_structure(self):\n        self.restart()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        for label in (\"主机\", \"端口\", \"请求\", \"路径\", \"用户\", \"密码\", \"HTTP\", \"HTTPS\", \"保存配置\"):\n            self.require_text(label, f\"配置页缺少 {label!r}\", timeout=3)\n        for key in (\"api_host_input\", \"api_port_input\", \"api_path_input\", \"api_user_input\",\n                    \"api_password_input\", \"api_protocol_http\", \"api_protocol_https\", \"api_save_button\"):\n            self.require_component(BY.key(key), f\"缺少组件 {key}\", timeout=3)\n        self.assertIn(self.status(), (\"正常\", \"不可用\"))\n        self.assertIsNotNone(self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"正常\"), 1) or self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"不可用\"), 1),\n                             \"状态值不是独立的 正常/不可用 文本\")\n        self.assertIsNone(self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"在线服务\"), 1), \"配置页内不应出现与选项同名的文本\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_save_button\"), \"\").isClickable())\n\n    # A11-A14：保存提示 + 真实检查请求 + 状态自动刷新（不可用 → 正常）\n    def test_02_save_toast_and_check(self):\n        self.restart()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=DEAD_PORT)\n        self.assertEqual(self.save(), T7)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"不可用\"), \"不可用\")\n\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT)\n        self.assertEqual(self.save(), T7)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\", \"检查通过后状态未自动变为 正常\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.checks(), 1, f\"保存一次应恰好发 1 次检查请求: {self.proxy.requests}\")\n        self.assertIsNone(self.proxy.auth_of(\"/inner/version\")[0], \"未配置用户/密码时不应带认证头\")\n\n    # A17：返回重进、冷启动后回显；冷启动自动检查\n    def test_03_persist_after_reenter_and_restart(self):\n        self.close_settings()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_host_input\"), HOST)\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_port_input\"), PORT)\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.restart()\n        self.assertTrue(self.wait_until(lambda: self.checks() >= 1, 10), \"冷启动未检查服务\")\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_host_input\"), HOST)\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_port_input\"), PORT)\n        self.assertTrue(self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_protocol_http\"), \"\").isChecked())\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.checks(), 1, \"进入配置页不应额外发检查请求\")\n\n    # A20：检查在途时连点保存，同一配置只发 1 次\n    def test_04_rapid_save_no_duplicate_request(self):\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.proxy.delay = 3.0\n        save_btn = self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_save_button\"), \"缺少保存按钮\")\n        for _ in range(3):\n            save_btn.click()\n            self.driver.wait(0.3)\n        self.driver.wait(4)\n        self.assertEqual(self.checks(), 1, f\"连点产生了重复请求: {self.proxy.paths('/inner/version')}\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\")\n\n    # A20：慢的旧检查（失败）晚返回，不得覆盖新配置的结果\n    def test_05_stale_result_ignored(self):\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.proxy.delay = 5.0\n        self.proxy.delay_prefix = \"/slow\"\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT, path=\"slow\")  # /slow/inner/version → 上游 404 → 不可用\n        self.save()\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT, path=\"\")\n        self.save()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\")\n        self.driver.wait(6)\n        self.assertEqual(self.proxy.paths(\"/inner/version\")[0].split(\"?\")[0], \"/slow/inner/version\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.status(), \"正常\", \"旧请求晚返回覆盖了新结果\")\n\n    # A16：用户/密码 → HTTP Basic 认证；清空后不再带\n    def test_06_basic_auth(self):\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT, user=\"tester\", password=\"p@ss\")\n        self.save()\n        self.assertTrue(self.wait_until(lambda: self.checks() >= 1, 10))\n        self.assertEqual(self.proxy.auth_of(\"/inner/version\")[-1], basic_auth(\"tester\", \"p@ss\"))\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT)\n        self.save()\n        self.assertTrue(self.wait_until(lambda: self.checks() >= 1, 10))\n        self.assertIsNone(self.proxy.auth_of(\"/inner/version\")[-1])\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\")\n\n    # A5/A15/A6：HTTPS 选择、路径规范化、非法端口\n    def test_07_protocol_path_port_normalization(self):\n        self.fill_form(host=\" http://\" + HOST + \"/ \", port=\"70000\", protocol=\"HTTPS\", path=\" /api/ \")\n        self.save()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"不可用\"), \"不可用\")\n        self.close_settings()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_host_input\"), HOST)\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_port_input\"), \"\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_path_input\"), \"/api\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_protocol_https\"), \"\").isChecked())\n        self.assertFalse(self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_protocol_http\"), \"\").isChecked())\n        # 点击选项文字也能切换\n        self.click_text(\"HTTPS\")\n        self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"HTTP\"), 2).click()\n        self.driver.wait(0.5)\n        self.assertTrue(self.require_component(BY.key(\"api_protocol_http\"), \"\").isChecked())\n\n    # A22：修改未保存即返回 → 不持久化、不发请求\n    def test_08_unsaved_changes_discarded(self):\n        self.fill_form(host=HOST, port=PORT)\n        self.save()\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"正常\"), \"正常\")\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.fill(\"api_host_input\", \"example.invalid\")\n        self.close_settings()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_host_input\"), HOST)\n        self.assertEqual(self.checks(), 0)\n        self.assertEqual(self.status(), \"正常\")\n\n    # A19：纯空格主机 → 允许保存（清除配置），提示 T7，不发请求，状态不可用\n    def test_09_blank_host_clears_config(self):\n        self.proxy.clear()\n        self.fill_form(host=\"   \", port=PORT)\n        self.assertEqual(self.save(), T7)\n        self.assertEqual(self.wait_status(\"不可用\", 5), \"不可用\")\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.assertEqual(self.checks(), 0, \"主机为空时不应发检查请求\")\n        self.restart()\n        self.open_api_page()\n        self.assertEqual(self.input_value(\"api_host_input\"), \"\")\n        self.assertEqual(self.status(), \"不可用\")\n        self.close_settings()\n"

     6→from hypium import BY

     8→DEFAULT_TIMEOUT = 15

    10→
    11→def restart_app(driver, bundle: str, wait_time: float = 2) -> None:
    12→    """强制停止后冷启动，等价于“完全退出再重新打开”。
    13→
    14→    签名取自 Hypium 6.1.0.210（stop_app / start_app），尚未在设备上实测；
    15→    首次使用时请确认 start_app 不带 page_name 时能拉起主 Ability。
    16→    """
    17→    driver.stop_app(bundle)
    18→    driver.start_app(bundle, wait_time=wait_time)
    19→
    20→
    21→def click_text_path(driver, texts: list[str], timeout: float = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT) -> None:
    22→    """依次点击一串文案，用于从某个起点页逐级导航到目标页。
    23→
    24→    每一步都等待目标文案出现；找不到时抛 AssertionError 并指出卡在哪一步。
    25→    """
    26→    for text in texts:
    27→        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    28→        component = None
    29→        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    30→            component = driver.find_component(BY.text(text))
    31→            if component is not None:
    32→                break
    33→            driver.wait(0.5)
    34→        if component is None:
    35→            raise AssertionError(f"导航失败：{timeout}s 内未找到文案 {text!r}")
    36→        driver.click(component)
    37→        driver.wait(0.5)

测试歌曲.mp3\"\n# MPEG-1 Layer III 128kbps 44.1kHz 单帧（417 字节）的静音数据\nMP3_FRAME = bytes([0xFF, 0xFB, 0x90, 0x64]) + bytes(413)\n\n\nclass TestS3LocalSongs(CmuTestCase):\n\n    def open_local_page(self) -> None:\n        self.click_text(\"我的\")\n        self.click_text(\"本地曲库\")\n        self.require_component(UPLOAD_MENU, \"未进入本地曲库页（缺少右上角上传入口）\")\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n\n    def count_text(self):\n        return self.find_or_none(BY.text(\"共 \", MatchPattern.STARTS_WITH), 3)\n\n    def push_song(self) -> None:\n        fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=\".mp3\")\n        with os.fdopen(fd, \"wb\") as f:\n            f.write(MP3_FRAME * 40)\n        try:\n            self.driver.push_file(path, f\"{DEVICE_DIR}/{SONG_FILE}\")\n        finally:\n            os.remove(path)\n\n    def delete_all_songs(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"多选 → 全选 → 删除 → 确认（基线 UI）。\"\"\"\n        count = self.count_text()\n        if count is None:\n            return\n        y = count.getBoundsCenter().y\n        row_images = [c for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Image\")) or [])\n                      if abs(c.getBoundsCenter().y - y) < 40]\n        max(row_images, key=lambda c: c.getBounds().left).click()  # 多选开关\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        self.require_component(BY.type(\"CheckboxGroup\"), \"未进入多选\").click()\n        self.driver.wait(0.8)\n        row_images = [c for c in (self.driver.find_all_components(BY.type(\"Image\")) or [])\n                      if abs(c.getBoundsCenter().y - y) < 40]\n        min(row_images, key=lambda c: c.getBounds().left).click()  # 删除\n        self.click_text(\"删除\", timeout=5)\n        self.driver.wait(1.5)\n\n    # C1/C2：空列表提示文案（单个 Text，真实换行）\n    def test_01_empty_tip(self):\n        self.restart()\n        self.open_local_page()\n        self.require_precondition(self.count_text() is None, \"本地曲库非空，无法验证空列表提示\")\n        tip = self.require_text(T12, f\"未找到空列表提示 {T12!r}\", timeout=5)\n        self.assertEqual(tip.getText(), T12)\n        self.assertIsNotNone(self.find_or_none(BY.key(\"local_empty_tip\"), 2))\n        self.assertTrue(self.require_component(UPLOAD_MENU, \"\").isClickable())\n        # 返回后重进仍显示\n        self.driver.press_back()\n        self.driver.wait(1)\n        self.click_text(\"本地曲库\")\n        self.require_text(T12, \"返回重进后空列表提示消失\", timeout=5)\n\n    # C3/C4：上传弹窗取消 → 列表不变\n    def test_02_upload_cancel(self):\n        self.require_component(UPLOAD_MENU, \"缺少上传入口\").click()\n        self.require_text(\"上传文件\", \"未弹出上传说明弹窗\", timeout=5)\n        self.click_text(\"取消\")\n        self.assertTrue(self.wait_gone(BY.text(\"上传文件\"), 5))\n        self.require_text(T12, \"取消后列表状态变化\", timeout=3)\n\n    # C4/C5/C6：选择本机音频 → 添加到列表；重启后仍在；删除后回到空提示\n    def test_03_upload_and_persist(self):\n        self.push_song()\n        try:\n            self.require_component(UPLOAD_MENU, \"缺少上传入口\").click()\n            self.click_text(\"上传\", timeout=5)\n            self.click_text(\"浏览\", timeout=15)\n            self.click_text(\"我的手机\", timeout=10)\n            self.click_text(\"Download\", timeout=10)\n            self.driver.start_listen_toast()\n            song = self.require_component(BY.text(\"测试歌手 - **[1.30 推理]**

```text
I'm digging into why test_03 failed after close_settings — it looks like the back navigation might be mishandling an open keyboard before returning to the settings list, which could explain why open_api_page can't find '在线服务' afterward.

I'm planning to add a debug script to inspect the actual UI layout at that point, then make close_settings more robust by looping on back presses until the settings panel elements, like the 'settings_online_service' id, are confirmed gone.

I should also guard against pressing back when nothing's open, since that could exit the app. For the cascading test failures, I'm thinking each test should ensure the config page is open at the start via a helper that restarts and reopens if needed, rather than relying on leftover state from prior tests. For open_api_page, I'll add a check to return early if the host input already exists, and handle closing any open sheet before clicking '我的'.


```

```text
I'm checking that open_api_page's early-return logic relies on the api_set_page NavDestination id, which doesn't exist in the explore sheet, so it falls through to close_settings behavior instead—pressing back to dismiss the sheet when api_host_input is present. I'm also noting that tests like test_03 and test_07 deliberately call close_settings first before re-entering, and checking how test_08 handles this pattern.

I'm also verifying that test_01's tree inspection correctly shows "在线服务" only in the list after navigating back, and reviewing whether keyboard overlap affects the save button clicks in earlier tests, which still passed. For robustness, I'm deciding to explicitly add an open_api_page() call at the start of tests 04 through 09, since it's a safe no-op if already on that page, rather than complicating setUp with conditional logic based on test names.


```

**[1.36 推理]**

```text
The second sheet open seems to have failed in test_03 — after dismissing via back and waiting for the sheet to disappear, clicking T10 didn't bring it back. I suspect a timing issue where the click happened before onDisappear fully reset isShowApiSheet, or the animation was still in progress when the next click fired.

This matches the trace: back press closes keyboard then sheet, wait_gone catches the early removal, then the next click on T10 fails because the sheet variable got clobbered before onDisappear reset it. The fix is to use onWillDisappear (API 12) to set the flag false at the start of dismissal, or switch to the $$ two-way binding for isShow which the SDK docs say has supported this since API 10 — I should check how Me.ets handles its settings sheet and whether SheetOptions actually exposes onWillDisappear in the type definitions.


```

**[1.39 推理]**

```text
I'm considering using `onWillDisappear` to reset the sheet flag before the close animation finishes, while keeping `onDisappear` too since setting it false twice is harmless. But I realize there's a race condition risk: if the user reopens the sheet during the closing animation, the flag could get set true then immediately false again, leaving the sheet state inconsistent.

So I'll remove `onDisappear` entirely and rely solely on `onWillDisappear` to avoid that mismatch. The existing flow where `ApiConfigForm`'s onSaved callback sets `isShowApiSheet` false should still work fine, since `onWillDisappear` just fires alongside it harmlessly.


```

1-do 这一步做完了。官方 runner 跑了 20 条自测，全部通过，0 失败、0 跳过。结果在 `logs/selftest.json` 和 `logs/selftest.log`，分场景的日志在 `logs/s1.log`、`s2.log`、`s3.log`。

**本次接着做的**
- 上次中断前，三个场景的功能代码基本已经写好，但只有 [`hypium_tests/cmu_helpers.py`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec1/hypium_tests/cmu_helpers.py)，还没有测试用例。
- 补了三个测试文件：
  - [`test_s1_api_config.py`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec1/hypium_tests/test_s1_api_config.py)：9 条
  - [`test_s2_explore_guide.py`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec1/hypium_tests/test_s2_explore_guide.py)：8 条
  - [`test_s3_local_songs.py`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec1/hypium_tests/test_s3_local_songs.py)：3 条
- 请求计数靠测试里自带的一个转发代理（宿主机 3901 端口转到 docker 的 3000），它会记录每个请求的路径和认证头。不直接看 `docker logs` 的原因：docker 服务有约 2 分钟的响应缓存，命中缓存的请求不写日志，会数漏。这一点已更新到记忆。
- 自测发现一个真实 bug，已修：发现页的配置面板返回关闭后，马上再点"设置在线服务"弹不出来。原因是状态要等关闭动画结束才复位。现在改为关闭动画开始前就复位（[`Explore.ets`](/Users/bress/Documents/code/harmonycup-work/cmu-spec1/entry/src/main/ets/common/view/Explore.ets) 里 `onDisappear` 换成 `onWillDisappear`）。

**自测覆盖的情况**
- 首次进入、无数据、未配置、服务不可用。
- 取消/返回不保存也不发请求；返回后重进、重启应用后，配置都能正确回显。
- 连点保存只发 1 次检查请求；先保存的慢请求晚返回，不会覆盖后来的结果。
- 主机只填空格时照常提示，但不发请求，状态显示"不可用"。
- 填了用户名/密码时请求带 Basic 认证，清空后不再带。
- 协议选 HTTPS、路径和端口的规范化。
- 本地曲库：上传 mp3、重启后仍在、删除后回到空列表提示。

**构建与清理**
- 最后做了一次 clean 构建，成功；告警按"文件 + 类别"和基线比，完全一致，没有新增。
- 最终的 HAP 已装到 127.0.0.1:5555。
- `git status` 只剩真实改动，没有 `BuildProfile.ets` 噪声。上次在工程根目录留下的 `reports/`、`tmp_hypium/` 和临时探针脚本已删除。

**已知问题和未覆盖项（给 2-submit 用）**
1. 没测"全新安装"。要测就得卸载重装，会清掉模拟器上这个 bundleName 的全部数据，而上游其他题共用这个 bundleName，所以没做。现在用"保存空主机"模拟未配置，走的代码路径基本相同。需要的话我可以在你确认后卸载重装再验证一次。
2. 旧版只存了一个 `apiUrl` 字符串时，解析回填到各字段的逻辑（验收项 A18）没有自动化测试，需要预置旧版的偏好设置数据。
3. 已配置但服务不可达时，发现页要等启动检查超时（最长 15 秒）才出现引导；未配置时约 1.5 秒内就出现。
4. 本地曲库的空列表文案是一个 Text、中间是真实换行。如果官方测试只按第一行"本地文件列表为空"做全等查找，会匹配不上（即已决的 U9 残余风险）。
5. 密码以明文存在应用沙箱的首选项 `Settings` 里，和基线保存 Cookie 的方式一样，没有加密。
6. 模拟器上当前的配置还指向 `10.0.2.2:3901`（测试代理，已停），打开应用会显示"不可用"，到配置页改回 `3000` 即可。

改动都还没提交，下一步可以进入 `2-submit.md`。

**[2.3 回复]**

当前会话的引擎 sessionUuid 是 `ecfd59f9-17e3-43b3-87b5-ea1858161860`。

侧边栏会话 ID 没查到。本工作区没有正在运行（`generating`）的 Spec 模式会话，也没有任何候选记录。侧边栏 ID 只有 Spec 模式会话才会记录，普通 Agent 会话通常没有。普通会话的侧边栏 ID 是否等于上面的 sessionUuid 没有验证过，不能当成同一个值。

依据：先打时间戳，再读 hook 日志 `~/.comate/builtin-hook-state/notify-dispatcher.log`。时间戳后 0.1 秒就有一条本工作区的工具调用记录，这条记录属于当前会话，期间没有别的会话在调用工具。侧边栏 ID 查的是 IDE 状态库 `state.vscdb`。
